- **Commit:** `9e4fc7b`, on top of `ca98e98`

## Changed
- `/Users/josemejia/Documents/Software Development/Qualitara/relay-activity-health/.claude/worktrees/agent-a8d10071812ab3a20/web/src/styles.scss`
- `.../web/src/app/features/dashboard/components/week-picker.ts`
- `.../web/src/app/features/dashboard/components/account-summary.ts`
- `.../web/src/app/features/dashboard/components/location-table.ts`
- `.../web/src/app/features/dashboard/components/dashboard-filters.ts`
- `.../web/src/app/features/dashboard/dashboard.page.ts`
- `.../web/src/app/features/dashboard/health-copy.ts`
- `.../web/src/app/features/dashboard/week.ts`
- `.../web/angular.json`

| Fix | Status |
|---|---|
| 1. Picker styles moved into WeekPicker | Applied. `encapsulation: ViewEncapsulation.None`; the dialog styles sit under `.week-picker-dialog`. The trigger's own styles had to move under `app-week-picker`, because `:host` doesn't work without encapsulation. Tokens, `mat.theme`, `theme-overrides` and `datepicker-overrides` stay global. The month-label workaround is unchanged, now as a one-line CSS comment. |
| 2. `statusIcon` | Applied. `statusIcon(status: HealthStatus): IconName \| null` sits next to `statusLabel`. Both templates use it via `@if (…; as iconName)`. |
| 3. Shared badge colours | Applied. One global `.status-badge[data-status='above'/'below']` rule. The table's badge `&lt;span&gt;` now carries `[attr.data-status]`. The summary's grey default fill is now scoped to `[data-status='normal']`, because a plain `.status-badge` rule in the component would have overridden the global colours. |
| 4. Card shell and controls | Applied. Global `.card`, a `.control` class holding the shared border, surface, ink, font and cursor, and one shared `.control:disabled` rule, plus the tokens `--radius-card`, `--radius-control` and `--control-height`. Used by the page cards, the summary card, the picker dialog, both selects, both step buttons, the trigger and "Latest week". |
| 5. Date filter | Applied. The filter keeps only `day !== null &amp;&amp; isMonday(day)`. `selectedWeekClass` uses `isSameWeek(..., { weekStartsOn: 1 }) &amp;&amp; !isMonday(day)`. `selectedSunday` and `isWithinInterval` are removed. |
| 6. `CdkTrapFocus` | Applied. The shared initial chunk shrank from 320.68 kB to 316.88 kB. |
| 7. `week.ts` formatters | Applied. A per-zone `Map` cache, with the UTC formatter created once at module level. The week arithmetic is untouched. |
| 8. Latin-only fonts | Applied. `@fontsource/geist/latin-400/500/600.css` exist in the installed 5.3.0 package. Global CSS went from 19.14 kB to 12.16 kB. |
| 9. Budget back to 4 kB | Skipped. The page's styles are 4.85 kB, so `maximumWarning` stays at 6 kB. |

**Component style sizes** (measured with a temporary 100 B budget, then angular.json restored):

| Component | Before | After |
|---|---|---|
| dashboard.page.ts | 4.89 kB | 4.85 kB |
| week-picker.ts | 0.94 kB | 3.49 kB (now includes the overlay styles) |
| dashboard-filters.ts | 1.82 kB | 1.60 kB |
| location-table.ts | 1.80 kB | 1.62 kB |
| account-summary.ts | 1.31 kB | 1.04 kB |

- `npm ci` → exit 0.
- `npm test -- --watch=false` → `Test Files 11 passed (11)`, `Tests 175 passed (175)`.

|---|---|
| account 14, 2026-07-20, all (1440×1220) | identical, bytes identical |
| picker open (1440×1220) | identical, bytes identical |
| account 6, 2026-06-29, lead_created (1440×1875) | identical, bytes identical |
| account 20 (1440×900) | identical, bytes identical |
| loading (1440×922) | identical, bytes identical |

## Deviations
- LocationTable keeps its card look on `:host`, using the `--radius-card` token, instead of adding the `.card` class to the host. On the host element, the page's scoped `.card { margin-top: 32px }` would beat `app-location-table { margin-top: 24px }` and move the table 8 px.
- The ingredients of the disabled look are unchanged, but the week trigger still overrides the shared disabled text colour with `--color-ink-2`, exactly as before.

## Uncertain
- `.control` and `.card` are extra classes in the markup. The specs don't select on them, but they are new DOM class attributes.
- No screenshot covers a disabled trigger (earliest week equals latest week). By specificity it matches the old rules: the fill and border come from `.control:disabled`, and `app-week-picker .trigger:disabled` keeps the text at ink-2.
- The latin-only font files have no `unicode-range`, so they match every character. Characters outside that file still fall back to the system font exactly as before, which the identical screenshots confirm.

```
    REDACT_FILE="$redact_file" perl -pe '
      BEGIN { open my $fh, "<", $ENV{REDACT_FILE} or die; chomp(@secrets = grep { /\S/ } <$fh>); }
      for my $secret (@secrets) { s/\Q$secret\E/<redacted>/g }
      s/(SA_PASSWORD[\\"=: ]+)[^\\"\s,]+/$1<redacted>/g;
    ' "$transcript" > "$target"
    rendered="$sessions_dir/${relative%.jsonl}.md"
    mkdir -p "$(dirname "$rendered")"
    render_markdown "$target" > "$rendered"
  done < <(find "$project" -name '*.jsonl' -not -path '*/memory/*')
done

echo "Exported $(find "$raw_dir" -name '*.jsonl' | wc -l | tr -d ' ') transcripts to ai-log/raw and ai-log/sessions"
```
</details>

<result>## Verdict: MERGE

I checked out 9e4fc7b in my worktree. `npm ci`, `npm test -- --watch=false` and `npm run build` all succeeded. The tests reported 11 files and 175 tests passing, and the build completed. I did not view the before/after screenshots.

## Findings (ranked, none blocking)

- [Minor] `web/src/app/features/dashboard/week.ts` (the `calendarDayFormatters` Map) — the cache is a module-level mutable Map keyed by IANA zone string. It is bounded in practice, because the only keys are 'UTC' plus the account timezones the API returns, which is a handful. It has no eviction and is not behind an interface. That is acceptable for a pure formatting helper, so I would not require a change. An invalid zone throws `RangeError` from the constructor and is not cached, which is the same as before.

- [Nit] There is no test for the new `statusIcon` export. This is not a violation, since the rules for this commit allow a new health-copy export. Existing component tests cover the rendered icons and they pass.

- [Nit] `week-picker.ts:~97` — the one-line why-comment moved from `//` in SCSS to `/* … */` in the component styles. It is still a single line, so it complies with CLAUDE.md.

## Requested checks

- **No test or public-contract edits.** The diff touches only `angular.json`, four components, `dashboard.page.ts`, `health-copy.ts`, `week.ts` and `styles.scss`. There are no spec files, and no changes to models, `IconName` or WeekPicker inputs and outputs. `statusIcon` is a new export only.

- **ViewEncapsulation.None scoping.**
  - Every unscoped rule is prefixed by `app-week-picker`, which is element-specific, or by `.week-picker-dialog`, which only the overlay div carries.
  - The keyframe name is `week-picker-open`, so it is unique.
  - The `.trigger` rules (specificity 0,2,1) still beat the global `.control:disabled` (0,2,0). The trigger's disabled `color: ink-2` therefore still applies.
  - Nothing leaks onto other elements.

- **Global additions.**
  - `.card` is now global. The dashboard page's `.card { margin-top }` rule is still view-scoped, so the dialog does not pick up a margin.
  - `.status-badge[data-status='above'|'below']` is now global. Both badges carry `data-status`, and the location-table badge moved the attribute onto itself from the `tr`.
  - The account-summary badge keeps its local `normal` and `insufficient_data` overrides. Those local rules have higher specificity than the global ones, so they still win.

- **dateFilter reduced to `isMonday`.** This is safe. `MatMonthView` enables a cell only when it is at or after `minDate`, at or before `maxDate`, and passes `dateFilter`. The bounds are `earliestMonday()` and `latestCompleteMonday()`, so out-of-range Mondays are still disabled.

- **`selectedWeekClass`.** `isSameWeek(day, selectedMonday, {weekStartsOn: 1}) &amp;&amp; !isMonday(day)` matches Tuesday to Sunday of the selected week. That is the same set as the old interval check.

- **CdkTrapFocus.** It is a standalone directive, and the import is correct.

- **Latin-only fonts.** The app's non-ASCII glyphs are `± · σ – — … ↑ → ↓ ▲ ▶ ▼ ◀`. `db/seed.sql` contains no non-ASCII characters, so no latin-ext glyphs are lost from data. The geometric arrow glyphs would come from fallback fonts either way.

- **CLAUDE.md rules.** No comment blocks. Names are descriptive: `statusIcon`, `calendarDayFormatter`, `utcCalendarDayFormatter`. No dead code. `selectedSunday` and `isWithinInterval` were removed cleanly.

## Hand-verified golden values
Not applicable. This commit changes no backend statistics code.</result>
